THE INSTITUTE OF CHARTERED ACCOUNTANTS OF 
INDIA 

New Delhi-l, the 9th July 1969 
(Chartered Accountants) 

No. 1-C.4 (30) 1/69 — In exercise of the powers con- 
ferred by Sub-section (1) of Section 30 of the Chartered 
Accountants Act, 1949, (XXXVIII of 1949) the Council 
of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of India has 
made the following amendments to the Chartered 
Accountants Regulations, 1964, the same having been pre- 
viously published and approved by the Central Govern- 
ment as required under Sub-scction (3) of the said Sec- 
tion. 

In the said Regulations : — 

Delete regulations 38 and 58 dealing respectively 
with the "Termination of Service” in the case of 
articled and audit clerks.
